# Target blueprint 28: Traceability And Coverage (part 3 of 4)

> **Status.** Archive: proposal, not ratified. A greenfield redesign blueprint written in October 2026. Every area was Draft, Under Review or Revision Required (its own document status) and no test was run against it. Its content was mined into `docs/blueprint/`; `SSOT.md` remains the source of intent. Module federation described here was not adopted: the built system is a modular monolith (`ARCHITECTURE.md`).
>
> **What this file holds.** All files of the former `Yash_Final_Project_Document/28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/` folder, in order.
>
> **Merged on 2026-11-25** from the files listed below; each one is a section under its original title. Nothing was removed; the originals are in git history at commit `08387ec`.

Parts: [part 1](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE.md) · [part 2](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART2.md) · [part 3](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART3.md) · [part 4](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART4.md)

## Coverage Matrices: AC to TEST, BR to AC to TEST, per-feature AC counts

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/COVERAGE_MATRICES.md`._

Doc ID: TRC-COV - Owner: QA Architect with Documentation Architect - Version 1.0 - Status: Draft - Date 2026-10-25 - Relates: TRC-01, QA-CAT, QA-CAT-R2, BR-REG, PCAP-01, REVW-17, REVW-36

**Relationship statement:** this file **supersedes nothing**. It supplements `AC_TEST_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md` (row-per-AC list, authored separately) with the business-rule chain, per-feature counts, tiering, blocker lists and the method. It is a derived mechanical audit; where it disagrees with a source table, the source table wins. Counts are valid for the files as read on 2026-10-25; other editors changed documents concurrently, so re-run the script (section 9) instead of trusting stale numbers.

### 1. Method

- Script: Python 3, regex over Markdown tables, source in section 9. Definition position = first cell of a table row.
- **AC defined:** first cell `AC-dd-ff-nn` in `05_*/SPEC_G1..G5` (long-form, 359) or in `16_*/ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA_GUIDE.md` (platform `AC-00-*`, 30). Duplicated definitions are merged.
- **AC to TEST (direct tier):** the AC id appears in any cell after the first of a row in `TEST_CATALOG.md` or `TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2`, **excluding** suite-level tests (`TEST-ACC-*`, `TEST-BRU-*`). Textual match only; semantic adequacy is not judged.
- **AC to FEAT:** `AC-dd-ff-nn` belongs to `FEAT-dd-ff`. This is the same convention as the independent review; an AC filed under a different FEAT number would be missed.
- **BR to TEST:** A = a direct-tier test row cites the BR. B = an AC row that cites the BR is itself directly tested. C = the BR's FEAT column names a feature whose ACs are directly tested, but no explicit BR link exists (indirect, unproven). D = none of the above.
- **Priority and phase** come from the last two cells of the FEAT row in the capability map. Those columns are being revised under REVW-10; re-run after the capability map is regenerated from the roadmap.
- Limits: prose citations without an id, ranges (`BR-T050...T059`) and shorthand are not resolved; test relevance is not judged; nothing here shows a test exists in code.

Discrepancies explained: capabilities are 108 `CAP-` rows (not 97); long-form ACs are 359 (358 published, one added later); tests are counted from catalog first cells (213 published omitted TEST-SEC-NEG-01; 210 counted the table header); work packages and ADRs moved because other editors added rows; the review's "158 features without an AC carrying their own FEAT number" is **168** by this script's convention (85 of 253 features have at least one AC), so TRC-01's note that the two definitions disagree is confirmed and the review figure is not reproduced.

Documents carrying superseded headline numbers (owners to correct; under the additive-only rule this pass did not edit them): PROJECT_MASTER s3, s4, s7; `04_*/CAPABILITY_MAP` L513; `16_*/TEST_CATALOG.md` s15; `28_*/TRACEABILITY_AND_VALIDATION.md` s2; `99_*/FINAL_DOMAIN_COVERAGE.md`; `99_*/IMPLEMENTATION_READINESS.md` ("26 features lack ACs" is 168 by script).

### 3. AC to TEST summary

| Platform AC-00-*        | 30      | 29                                   | 1                  | 0                                          | 1 (AC-00-01-07: register names TEST-DAT-008) |

Not-directly-cited ACs by feature priority, phase and suite status:

| Priority | Phase | Count with a REV2 suite | Count with no test of any tier |

| AC       | -     | 0                       | 1                              |
| P0       | 1     | 128                     | 0                              |
| P0       | 2     | 61                      | 0                              |

Honest reading: only 97 of 359 long-form criteria (27%) are cited by an individual test. The REV2 suites raise tier-2 coverage to 286 but they are table-driven placeholders, not authored tests. P1 criteria remain without any test and must be covered before their feature's phase exit.

### 4. Suite-level coverage rule

A criterion is _covered_ for a Ready decision only when an individual test cites it. A criterion tied only by `TEST-ACC-*` is reported as _suite-covered_ and may not be counted in the coverage headline. The suites are defined in `TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2` section 3.

#### 5.1 Blockers to Ready: P0 features with no AC

Rule (REVW-17): a P0 feature with no AC **and** no test cannot be Ready. A P0 feature with no AC but a feature-level test is marked _partial_; it still needs ACs before Ready.

### 9. Script source (reproducibility)

#### trace_lib.py

```python
"""Shared parsing for the TalentSphere documentation traceability scripts (REVW-17/36)."""
import re, pathlib, collections, json
ROOT = pathlib.Path(r"C:/Users/yashs/OneDrive/Desktop/talentsphere-complete/Yash_Final_Project_Document")
AC_RE = re.compile(r"AC-\d\d-\d\d-\d\d")
BR_RE = re.compile(r"BR-T\d{3}")
FEAT_RE = re.compile(r"FEAT-\d\d-\d\d")
TEST_RE = re.compile(r"TEST-[A-Z0-9]+(?:-[A-Z0-9]+)+")

def read(rel):
    return (ROOT / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

def rows(rel):
    """Yield (first_cell_clean, cells, raw_line) for markdown table rows."""
    for line in read(rel).splitlines():
        if not line.startswith("|"):
            continue
        cells = [c.strip() for c in line.strip().strip("|").split("|")]
        if not cells:
            continue
        yield re.sub(r"[`*]", "", cells[0]), cells, line

def all_md():
    return sorted(p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix() for p in ROOT.rglob("*.md"))

def load():
    D = {}
    # --- features, priority, phase (04 capability map)
    feats = {}
    micro = set()
    for c0, cells, line in rows("04_PRODUCT_CAPABILITIES/CAPABILITY_MAP_AND_FEATURE_HIERARCHY.md"):
        m = re.match(r"(FEAT-\d\d-\d\d)$", c0)
        if m and len(cells) >= 6:
            pri, ph = cells[-2], cells[-1]
            feats[m.group(1)] = {"name": cells[2][:80], "pri": pri, "phase": ph}
            for mm in re.finditer(r"(?:^|\s)\.(\d+)\s", cells[2]):
                micro.add((m.group(1), mm.group(1)))
    D["feats"] = feats
    D["micro"] = micro
    caps = set()
    for c0, cells, line in rows("04_PRODUCT_CAPABILITIES/CAPABILITY_MAP_AND_FEATURE_HIERARCHY.md"):
        if re.match(r"CAP-\d\d-\d+$", c0):
            caps.add(c0)
    D["caps"] = caps
    # --- ACs: long-form in 05 SPEC_G*, platform in 16 ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA_GUIDE (AC-00-*)
    acs = {}  # id -> {src, text}
    for rel in all_md():
        if rel.startswith("05_FEATURE_SPECIFICATIONS/SPEC_G") or rel == "16_QA_AND_TESTING/ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA_GUIDE.md":
            for c0, cells, line in rows(rel):
                m = AC_RE.match(c0)
                if m:
                    ac = m.group(0)
                    if ac not in acs:
                        acs[ac] = {"src": rel, "text": line}
                    else:
                        acs[ac]["text"] += " " + line
    D["acs"] = acs
    # --- BRs
    brs = {}
    for c0, cells, line in rows("06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/BUSINESS_RULES_REGISTRY.md"):
        if re.match(r"BR-T\d{3}$", c0):
            feat_cell = cells[6] if len(cells) > 6 else ""
            brs[c0] = {"scope": cells[1] if len(cells) > 1 else "", "feat_cell": feat_cell, "line": line}
    D["brs"] = brs
    # --- tests
    tests = {}
    for tcat in ("16_QA_AND_TESTING/TEST_CATALOG.md", "16_QA_AND_TESTING/TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2"):
        if not (ROOT / tcat).exists():
            continue
        for c0, cells, line in rows(tcat):
            m = re.match(r"(TEST-[A-Z0-9]+(?:-[A-Z0-9]+)+)", c0)
            if m:
                tests.setdefault(m.group(1), {"line": line, "cells": cells, "src": tcat})
    D["tests"] = tests
    # --- other registries (first-cell definitions)
    def defs(pattern, files):
        s = set()
        for rel in files:
            for c0, cells, line in rows(rel):
                m = re.match(pattern, c0)
                if m:
                    s.add(m.group(0))
        return s
    D["inv"] = defs(r"INV-T\d{2}$", ["00_CONTROL_CENTRE/CANONICAL_INVARIANTS.md", "06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/PRODUCT_INVARIANT_REGISTRY.md"])
    D["ff"] = defs(r"FF-\d\d$", ["00_CONTROL_CENTRE/CANONICAL_INVARIANTS.md"])
    D["bp"] = defs(r"BP-[A-Z0-9-]+$", ["06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/BUSINESS_PROCESS_INVENTORY.md"])
    D["dt"] = defs(r"DT-\d\d$", ["06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/DECISION_TABLES.md"])
    D["sm"] = defs(r"SM-[A-Z]+$", ["06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/STATE_MACHINES.md"])
    D["uj"] = defs(r"UJ-\d\d$", ["02_STAKEHOLDERS_AND_USERS/USER_JOURNEYS.md"])
    return D
```

#### build_matrices.py

```python
"""Build AC->TEST, BR->AC->TEST and per-feature AC matrices (REVW-17) and headline counts (REVW-36).
Usage: python build_matrices.py [--write]   (prints JSON summary; --write regenerates COVERAGE_MATRICES.md)"""
import re, sys, json, collections, datetime
import trace_lib as T
D = T.load()
feats, acs, brs, tests = D["feats"], D["acs"], D["brs"], D["tests"]

def first_cell_ids(rel, pat):
    return {m.group(0) for c0, _, _ in T.rows(rel) for m in [re.match(pat, c0)] if m}
def heading_ids(rel, pat):
    return set(re.findall(pat, T.read(rel), flags=re.M))

H = collections.OrderedDict()
H["Domains (DOM)"] = len(first_cell_ids("00_CONTROL_CENTRE/ID_REGISTRY.md", r"DOM-\d\d$"))
H["Capabilities (CAP)"] = len(D["caps"])
H["Features (FEAT)"] = len(feats)
H["Micro-features (FEAT-dd-nn.k)"] = len(D["micro"])
H["Long-form ACs (SPEC-G1..G5)"] = sum(1 for a in acs.values() if "SPEC_G" in a["src"])
H["Platform ACs (AC-00-*)"] = sum(1 for a in acs if a.startswith("AC-00-"))
H["Business rules (BR-T)"] = len(brs)
H["Invariants (INV-T)"] = len(D["inv"])
H["Fitness functions (FF)"] = len(D["ff"])
H["State machines (SM)"] = len(heading_ids("06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/STATE_MACHINES.md", r"^## (SM-[A-Z]+) "))
H["Decision tables (DT)"] = len(heading_ids("06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/DECISION_TABLES.md", r"^## (DT-\d\d) "))
H["Business processes (BP)"] = len(D["bp"])
H["User journeys (UJ)"] = len(D["uj"])
H["Screens (SCR)"] = len(first_cell_ids("07_UI_UX_AND_DESIGN_SYSTEM/SCREEN_REGISTRY.md", r"SCR-[A-Z]+-\d+"))
H["Actions (ACT)"] = len(first_cell_ids("07_UI_UX_AND_DESIGN_SYSTEM/ACTION_REGISTRY.md", r"ACT-[A-Z]+-\d+"))
H["API operations in catalog"] = len(first_cell_ids("13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/API_CATALOG.md", r"API-[A-Z0-9-]+"))
H["Events (EVT)"] = len(first_cell_ids("13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/EVENT_CATALOG.md", r"EVT-[A-Z0-9.-]+"))
H["Runbooks (RB)"] = len(first_cell_ids("18_OPERATIONS_AND_SUPPORT/RUNBOOK_INDEX.md", r"RB-\d+"))
H["Risks (RISK)"] = len(first_cell_ids("24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS/RISK_REGISTER.md", r"RISK-\d+"))
H["ADRs (ADR-T)"] = len(first_cell_ids("24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS/ADR_REGISTER.md", r"ADR-T\d+[a-z]?"))
H["Work packages (WP)"] = len(first_cell_ids("22_IMPLEMENTATION_ROADMAP/ROADMAP.md", r"WP-[A-Z0-9-]+"))
H["Tests (TEST)"] = len(tests)

# ---- test -> cited ids
t_ac = collections.defaultdict(set); t_br = collections.defaultdict(set); t_feat = collections.defaultdict(set)
t_inv = collections.defaultdict(set); t_ff = collections.defaultdict(set)
for tid, t in tests.items():
    body = " ".join(t["cells"][1:])
    for a in T.AC_RE.findall(body): t_ac[a].add(tid)
    for b in T.BR_RE.findall(body): t_br[b].add(tid)
    for f in T.FEAT_RE.findall(body): t_feat[f].add(tid)
    for i in re.findall(r"INV-T\d\d", body): t_inv[i].add(tid)
    for f in re.findall(r"FF-\d\d", body): t_ff[f].add(tid)
unlinked = [tid for tid, t in tests.items() if not re.search(r"(AC-\d|FEAT-|INV-T|BR-T|FF-|ADR-T|FM-|SLO-|OPS-|RB-|OPEN-|TP-|RMP|WCAG|FD-|SEC-|PWA-|STR-|MOB-|PLT-|DEP-|DATA-|PRIV|CSC|BE-|DB-|EVT-|API-|RISK|SM-|DT-|PUR-|DOM-|GOAL|MET-|THR|STRIDE|ABUSE)", " ".join(t["cells"][1:]))]

# ---- AC -> TEST
reverse_register = {}  # AC-00 register names a TEST
for a, v in acs.items():
    if a.startswith("AC-00-"):
        reverse_register[a] = set(T.TEST_RE.findall(v["text"])) & set(tests)
ac_cov = {a: sorted(t_ac.get(a, [])) for a in acs}
ac_uncovered = sorted(a for a, v in ac_cov.items() if not v)
ac_uncov_reg_ok = sorted(a for a in ac_uncovered if reverse_register.get(a))

# ---- per-feature
def fkey(a):
    m = re.match(r"AC-(\d\d)-(\d\d)-\d\d", a)
    return f"FEAT-{m.group(1)}-{m.group(2)}" if m and m.group(1) != "00" else None
f_acs = collections.defaultdict(list)
for a in acs:
    k = fkey(a)
    if k: f_acs[k].append(a)
ac_unknown_feat = sorted(a for a in acs if fkey(a) and fkey(a) not in feats)
feat_rows = []
for f, meta in sorted(feats.items()):
    al = sorted(f_acs.get(f, []))
    cov = [a for a in al if ac_cov[a]]
    feat_rows.append(dict(feat=f, pri=meta["pri"], phase=meta["phase"], n_ac=len(al), n_ac_tested=len(cov),
                          n_test_feat=len(t_feat.get(f, [])), n_test_any=len({t for a in al for t in ac_cov[a]} | t_feat.get(f, set()))))
no_ac = [r for r in feat_rows if r["n_ac"] == 0]
p0_no_ac = [r for r in no_ac if r["pri"].startswith("P0")]
p0_no_ac_no_test = [r for r in p0_no_ac if r["n_test_feat"] == 0]
p0_ph1_no_ac = [r for r in p0_no_ac if r["phase"].strip() == "1"]
no_ac_no_test = [r for r in no_ac if r["n_test_feat"] == 0]
feats_no_direct_test = [r for r in feat_rows if r["n_test_any"] == 0]

# ---- BR -> AC -> TEST
ac_text_br = collections.defaultdict(set)   # BR -> ACs whose row cites it
for a, v in acs.items():
    for b in T.BR_RE.findall(v["text"]): ac_text_br[b].add(a)
def feat_tokens(cell):
    out = set(T.FEAT_RE.findall(cell))
    for m in re.finditer(r"FEAT-(\d\d)-\*", cell):
        out |= {f for f in feats if f.startswith(f"FEAT-{m.group(1)}-")}
    return out
br_rows = []
for b, v in sorted(brs.items()):
    direct = sorted(t_br.get(b, []))
    via_ac = sorted({a for a in ac_text_br.get(b, []) if ac_cov[a]})
    fe = feat_tokens(v["feat_cell"])
    feat_level_acs = sorted({a for f in fe for a in f_acs.get(f, []) if ac_cov[a]})
    if direct: status = "A direct TEST"
    elif via_ac: status = "B via AC"
    elif feat_level_acs: status = "C feature-level only"
    else: status = "D none"
    br_rows.append(dict(br=b, scope=v["scope"], feats=sorted(fe), direct=direct, via_ac=via_ac, n_featacs=len(feat_level_acs), status=status))
br_cnt = collections.Counter(r["status"] for r in br_rows)

# ---- INV / FF coverage
inv_unc = sorted(i for i in D["inv"] if not t_inv.get(i))
ff_all = sorted(D["ff"]); ff_unc = sorted(f for f in ff_all if not t_ff.get(f))

S = dict(date=str(datetime.date.today()), headline=H, n_ac=len(acs), n_ac_uncovered=len(ac_uncovered), n_ac_reg_named=len(ac_uncov_reg_ok),
         n_feat_no_ac=len(no_ac), p0_no_ac=len(p0_no_ac), p0_no_ac_no_test=len(p0_no_ac_no_test), p0_ph1_no_ac=len(p0_ph1_no_ac),
         br=dict(br_cnt), inv_uncovered=inv_unc, ff_uncovered=ff_unc, tests_unlinked=unlinked, ac_unknown_feat=ac_unknown_feat,
         n_feat_no_direct_test=len(feats_no_direct_test))
if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(json.dumps(S, indent=1))
```

#### generate_outputs.py

````python
"""Generate 28_*/COVERAGE_MATRICES.md and 16_*/TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2 (additive new files). Usage: python generate_outputs.py"""
import re, collections, pathlib, datetime
import build_matrices as B
import trace_lib as T

ROOT = T.ROOT
today = str(datetime.date.today())
feats, acs, brs, tests = B.feats, B.acs, B.brs, B.tests
SUITE = ("TEST-ACC-", "TEST-BRU-")

# ---------- direct tier = every test except suite-level ones (rebuild from B's tables, excluding suites)
def is_suite(t): return t.startswith(SUITE)
t_ac = collections.defaultdict(set); t_br = collections.defaultdict(set); t_feat = collections.defaultdict(set)
for tid, t in tests.items():
    if is_suite(tid): continue
    body = " ".join(t["cells"][1:])
    for a in T.AC_RE.findall(body): t_ac[a].add(tid)
    for b in T.BR_RE.findall(body): t_br[b].add(tid)
    for f in T.FEAT_RE.findall(body): t_feat[f].add(tid)
ac_direct = {a: sorted(t_ac.get(a, [])) for a in acs}
ac_unc = sorted(a for a, v in ac_direct.items() if not v)
fk = B.fkey

# ---------- AC suites: one TEST-ACC per P0 feature with uncovered ACs
byf = collections.defaultdict(list)
for a in ac_unc:
    k = fk(a)
    if k: byf[k].append(a)
suite_rows, suite_of_ac = [], {}
for f, lst in sorted(byf.items()):
    meta = feats[f]
    if not meta["pri"].startswith("P0"):
        continue
    sid = "TEST-ACC-" + f[5:]
    src = sorted({acs[a]["src"].split("/")[-1] for a in lst})[0]
    auto = "A1" if meta["phase"].strip() == "1" else "A2"
    suite_rows.append("| %s | Given the acceptance table of %s in `%s`, when each of its %d AC rows that no individual test cites is executed table-driven at its stated Type, then every row passes and a failing row fails the suite naming its AC id (suite-level link; see COVERAGE_MATRICES section 4) | I | %s | %s |"
                      % (sid, f, src, len(lst), ", ".join(lst), auto))
    for a in lst: suite_of_ac[a] = sid

# ---------- BR rule suites by numbering block
blocks = [("T001", "T040", "core loop"), ("T041", "T049", "privileged access and safety"), ("T050", "T059", "identity and sessions"),
          ("T060", "T069", "privacy and data rights"), ("T070", "T077", "matching and search"), ("T080", "T087", "messaging and notifications"),
          ("T090", "T097", "billing and entitlements"), ("T100", "T106", "institutions"), ("T110", "T117", "AI and platform")]
ac_text_br = collections.defaultdict(set)
for a, v in acs.items():
    for b in T.BR_RE.findall(v["text"]): ac_text_br[b].add(a)
def feat_tokens(cell):
    out = set(T.FEAT_RE.findall(cell))
    for m in re.finditer(r"FEAT-(\d\d)-\*", cell):
        out |= {f for f in feats if f.startswith("FEAT-%s-" % m.group(1))}
    return out
f_acs = collections.defaultdict(list)
for a in acs:
    if fk(a): f_acs[fk(a)].append(a)
br_info = {}
for b, v in sorted(brs.items()):
    direct = sorted(t_br.get(b, []))
    via_ac = sorted(a for a in ac_text_br.get(b, []) if ac_direct[a])
    fe = feat_tokens(v["feat_cell"])
    fl = sorted(a for f in fe for a in f_acs.get(f, []) if ac_direct[a])
    st = "A direct TEST" if direct else "B via cited AC" if via_ac else "C feature-level only" if fl else "D none"
    br_info[b] = dict(direct=direct, via_ac=via_ac, fl=len(fl), status=st, feats=sorted(fe))
bru_rows, suite_of_br = [], {}
for lo, hi, name in blocks:
    members = [b for b in sorted(brs) if lo <= b[3:] <= hi and br_info[b]["status"] in ("C feature-level only", "D none")]
    if not members: continue
    sid = "TEST-BRU-%s-%s" % (lo, hi)
    bru_rows.append("| %s | Given the %s business rules %s that no individual test cites, when each rule is exercised by the verification type declared in its `Verification` column of `BUSINESS_RULES_REGISTRY.md` (UT, IT, SEC, E2E, PROP or CAN), then each rule's behaviour and exception hold and a failing rule fails the suite naming its BR id (suite-level link) | I | %s | A1 |"
                    % (sid, name, "%d rules" % len(members), ", ".join(members)))
    for b in members: suite_of_br[b] = sid

# ---------- TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2
cat = (ROOT / "16_QA_AND_TESTING/TEST_CATALOG.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
NEWIDS = ["TEST-SEC-032", "TEST-SEC-033", "TEST-SEC-034", "TEST-SEC-035", "TEST-EVD-016", "TEST-EVD-017", "TEST-EVD-018", "TEST-API-015", "TEST-API-016",
          "TEST-PRV-011", "TEST-PRV-012", "TEST-PRV-013", "TEST-SRC-003", "TEST-REL-013", "TEST-BIL-009", "TEST-OBS-007", "TEST-OBS-008",
          "TEST-PWA-013", "TEST-PWA-014", "TEST-PWA-015", "TEST-FED-016", "TEST-FED-017"]
rowtxt = {}
for line in cat.splitlines():
    m = re.match(r"\| (TEST-[A-Z0-9]+-\d+)", line)
    if m and m.group(1) in NEWIDS: rowtxt[m.group(1)] = line.rstrip()
missing = [i for i in NEWIDS if i not in rowtxt]
cond_fed = ["TEST-FED-%03d" % i for i in (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9,12,13,14,15)] + ["TEST-SEC-013", "TEST-SEC-014", "TEST-PER-004"]
cond_out = ["TEST-PWA-005", "TEST-PWA-006", "TEST-PWA-008", "TEST-PWA-014"]
groups = [("Negative control and security (REVW-01 already in catalog as TEST-SEC-NEG-01; REVW-32)", ["TEST-SEC-032","TEST-SEC-033","TEST-SEC-034","TEST-SEC-035"]),
          ("Instructor is not the learner; issuer class shown (REVW-12)", ["TEST-EVD-016","TEST-EVD-017","TEST-EVD-018"]),
          ("Idempotency and vocabulary lint (REVW-14, FF-07, FF-11)", ["TEST-API-015","TEST-API-016"]),
          ("Privacy: retention, minimum age, consent vocabulary (REVW-26, REVW-29, FF-12)", ["TEST-PRV-011","TEST-PRV-012","TEST-PRV-013"]),
          ("No consent cache at read time (REVW-25)", ["TEST-SRC-003"]),
          ("Resilience (REVW-41)", ["TEST-REL-013"]),
          ("First-party courses only in Ph2 (REVW-30)", ["TEST-BIL-009"]),
          ("Synthetic-only client SLIs and operational telemetry class (REVW-31)", ["TEST-OBS-007","TEST-OBS-008"]),
          ("Offline scope: drafts only in Ph1, lesson progress conditional, assessment answers online-only (REVW-24, REVW-15)", ["TEST-PWA-013","TEST-PWA-014","TEST-PWA-015"]),
          ("Bundled default and federated activation gate (REVW-07, REVW-08)", ["TEST-FED-016","TEST-FED-017"])]
R = []
R.append("# Test Catalog Revision 2 (additive)\n")
R.append("Doc ID: QA-CAT-R2 - Owner: QA Architect - Version 1.0 - Status: Draft for Review - Date %s - Relates: QA-CAT, REVIEW_RESOLUTION_DECISIONS (REV-RES), TRC-COV (`28_*/COVERAGE_MATRICES.md`)\n" % today)
R.append("**Relationship statement:** this file **supersedes nothing**. It is an additive companion to `TEST_CATALOG.md`. It records (1) tests added for the binding review decisions, (2) the conditional-tag table, (3) suite-level tests that tie otherwise uncited P0 acceptance criteria and business rules to an executable check, generated by script. All tests are *specified*, none implemented. Test IDs here continue the numbering of `TEST_CATALOG.md`; no ID is reused.\n")
R.append("**In-place edits disclosure:** during an earlier pass, rows for the tests in section 1 and the `[COND-FED]` / `[COND-OUTBOX]` tags of section 2 were also written into `TEST_CATALOG.md`; that was before the additive-only rule. The text here is identical for the section 1 rows. If those in-place edits are reverted by the owner, this file stands alone and the IDs stay valid.\n")
R.append("## 1. New tests for review decisions\n")
R.append("| TEST-ID | Verifies (Given / When / Then) | Lvl | Links | Auto |\n|---|---|---|---|---|")
for title, ids in groups:
    R.append("| **%s** | | | | |" % title)
    for i in ids:
        R.append(rowtxt.get(i, "| %s | (row text missing from TEST_CATALOG.md at generation time) | | | |" % i))
R.append("")

R.append("`[COND-FED]` = runs only once federated runtime is activated (`MF_MODE=federated`; ADR-T03a: OPEN-03 closed with at least 3 named independent frontend teams and a recorded `WP-FED-SPIKE` result). In the default `bundled` mode they are not run and do not gate Phase 1 exit. `[COND-OUTBOX]` = runs only once the offline outbox subsystem is activated on the ADR-T10 trigger (telemetry or research shows need). Phase 1 offline is read cache plus local unsent drafts, no outbox (TEST-PWA-013).\n")
R.append("| Tag | Existing tests that become conditional | Replacement unconditional gate for Phase 1 |\n|---|---|---|")
R.append("| COND-FED | %s | TEST-FED-010 (bundled e2e), TEST-FED-016 (bundled chunks and back-office edge rule), TEST-FED-017 (activation gate), TEST-SEC-012 and TEST-FED-011 (boundary lint and tracing apply to modules in either mode) |" % ", ".join(cond_fed))
R.append("| COND-OUTBOX | %s | TEST-PWA-004, TEST-PWA-007 (stale labelling, consequential actions blocked offline), TEST-PWA-013 (local drafts, no outbox), TEST-PWA-015 (assessment answers online-only) |" % ", ".join(cond_out))
R.append("")
R.append("Rewording of existing rows (disclosed, cannot be additive): TEST-FED-010 now states bundled mode is the Phase 1 gate; TEST-API-003 now reads \"accepts `Idempotency-Key` or declares a natural key\" (REVW-14). The authoritative wording is TEST-API-015 and TEST-FED-010 as written in this file's section 1 and in the Phase 1 gate column above.\n")
R.append("## 3. Suite-level acceptance tests for uncited P0 acceptance criteria (generated)\n")
R.append("Generated %s by `generate_outputs.py` from the AC tables in `05_*/SPEC_G*.md`. One suite per **P0 feature** that has acceptance criteria no individual test cites. A suite is a **suite-level link**: it makes the criteria executable as table-driven checks but is weaker than an individual test, and `COVERAGE_MATRICES.md` reports the two tiers separately. P1 features' uncited criteria are listed there but get no suite (they ship with their phase).\n" % today)
R.append("| TEST-ID | Verifies | Lvl | Links | Auto |\n|---|---|---|---|---|")

Each rule already declares its verification type in `BUSINESS_RULES_REGISTRY.md`; the suite makes that obligation a catalog entry.\n")

         % (n_new, len(suite_rows), len(bru_rows), n_new + len(suite_rows) + len(bru_rows), len([t for t in tests if t not in set(NEWIDS) and not is_suite(t)]) + 0, len([t for t in tests if not is_suite(t)])))
if missing: R.append("Generation warning: missing rows %s\n" % missing)
(ROOT / "16_QA_AND_TESTING/TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2").write_text("\n".join(R), encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")

# ---------- COVERAGE_MATRICES.md
H = B.H
claims = [("Capabilities", "97 (PROJECT_MASTER, CAPABILITY_MAP L513)", H["Capabilities (CAP)"]),

          ("Fitness functions FF", "10 (TRC-01; FF-11 and FF-12 added since)", H["Fitness functions (FF)"]), ("State machines", "15 (TRC-01 mechanical scan reported 19; not reproduced)", H["State machines (SM)"]),
          ("Decision tables", "12", H["Decision tables (DT)"]), ("Business processes", "38", H["Business processes (BP)"]),
          ("Screens", "130", H["Screens (SCR)"]), ("Actions", "231", H["Actions (ACT)"]), ("API operations (catalog)", "230", H["API operations in catalog"]),
          ("Events", "74", H["Events (EVT)"]), ("Runbooks", "37", H["Runbooks (RB)"]), ("Risks", "50", H["Risks (RISK)"]),
          ("ADRs (ADR-T)", "22", H["ADRs (ADR-T)"]), ("Work packages", "56", H["Work packages (WP)"]),
          ("Individual tests (catalog + REV2)", "213 (TEST_CATALOG s15, PROJECT_MASTER), 210 (TRC-01)", len([t for t in tests if not is_suite(t)]))]
nlf = H["Long-form ACs (SPEC-G1..G5)"]
lf = [a for a, v in acs.items() if "SPEC_G" in v["src"]]
lf_unc = [a for a in lf if not ac_direct[a]]
a00 = [a for a in acs if a.startswith("AC-00-")]
a00_unc = [a for a in a00 if not ac_direct[a]]
reg_named = [a for a in a00_unc if B.reverse_register.get(a)]
tier_cnt = collections.Counter()
for a in ac_unc:
    k = fk(a); m = feats.get(k, {"pri": "?", "phase": "?"}) if k else {"pri": "AC-00", "phase": "-"}
    tier_cnt[(m["pri"][:2], m["phase"].strip(), "suite" if a in suite_of_ac else "none")] += 1
feat_rows = []
for f, meta in sorted(feats.items()):
    al = sorted(f_acs.get(f, [])); cov = [a for a in al if ac_direct[a]]
    feat_rows.append((f, meta["pri"], meta["phase"].strip(), len(al), len(cov), len(t_feat.get(f, []))))

L = []

L.append("**Relationship statement:** this file **supersedes nothing**. It supplements `AC_TEST_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md` (row-per-AC list, authored separately) with the business-rule chain, per-feature counts, tiering, blocker lists and the method. Counts are valid for the files as read on %s; other editors changed documents concurrently, so re-run the script (section 9) instead of trusting stale numbers.\n" % today)
L.append("## 1. Method\n")

L.append("- **AC defined:** first cell `AC-dd-ff-nn` in `05_*/SPEC_G1..G5` (long-form, %d) or in `16_*/ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA_GUIDE.md` (platform `AC-00-*`, %d). Duplicated definitions are merged." % (len(lf), len(a00)))
L.append("- **AC to TEST (direct tier):** the AC id appears in any cell after the first of a row in `TEST_CATALOG.md` or `TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2`, **excluding** suite-level tests (`TEST-ACC-*`, `TEST-BRU-*`). Textual match only; semantic adequacy is not judged.")
L.append("- **AC to FEAT:** `AC-dd-ff-nn` belongs to `FEAT-dd-ff`. This is the same convention as the independent review; an AC filed under a different FEAT number would be missed.")
L.append("- **BR to TEST:** A = a direct-tier test row cites the BR. B = an AC row that cites the BR is itself directly tested. C = the BR's FEAT column names a feature whose ACs are directly tested, but no explicit BR link exists (indirect, unproven). D = none of the above.")
L.append("- **Priority and phase** come from the last two cells of the FEAT row in the capability map. Those columns are being revised under REVW-10; re-run after the capability map is regenerated from the roadmap.")

L.append("| Item | Previously published | By script %s |\n|---|---|---|" % today)
for a, b, c in claims: L.append("| %s | %s | %s |" % (a, b, c))

L.append("Documents carrying superseded headline numbers (owners to correct; under the additive-only rule this pass did not edit them): PROJECT_MASTER s3, s4, s7; `04_*/CAPABILITY_MAP` L513; `16_*/TEST_CATALOG.md` s15; `28_*/TRACEABILITY_AND_VALIDATION.md` s2; `99_*/FINAL_DOMAIN_COVERAGE.md`; `99_*/IMPLEMENTATION_READINESS.md` (\"26 features lack ACs\" is %d by script).\n" % len(no_ac))
L.append("## 3. AC to TEST summary\n")
L.append("| Scope | Defined | Directly cited by an individual test | Not directly cited | Of which tied by a suite-level test (REV2) | Still uncovered |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
lf_suite = sum(1 for a in lf_unc if a in suite_of_ac); a00_suite = sum(1 for a in a00_unc if a in suite_of_ac)
L.append("| Long-form (SPEC-G1..G5) | %d | %d | %d | %d | %d |" % (len(lf), len(lf) - len(lf_unc), len(lf_unc), lf_suite, len(lf_unc) - lf_suite))
L.append("| Platform AC-00-* | %d | %d | %d | %d | %d (%s) |" % (len(a00), len(a00) - len(a00_unc), len(a00_unc), a00_suite, len(a00_unc) - a00_suite, ", ".join("%s: register names %s" % (a, ", ".join(sorted(B.reverse_register[a]))) for a in reg_named) or "none"))
L.append("| Total | %d | %d | %d | %d | %d |\n" % (len(acs), len(acs) - len(ac_unc), len(ac_unc), len(suite_of_ac), len(ac_unc) - len(suite_of_ac)))
L.append("Not-directly-cited ACs by feature priority, phase and suite status:\n")
L.append("| Priority | Phase | Count with a REV2 suite | Count with no test of any tier |\n|---|---|---|---|")
keys = sorted({(k[0], k[1]) for k in tier_cnt})
for p, ph in keys:
    L.append("| %s | %s | %d | %d |" % (p, ph, tier_cnt[(p, ph, "suite")], tier_cnt[(p, ph, "none")]))
L.append("\nHonest reading: only %d of %d long-form criteria (%.0f%%) are cited by an individual test. The REV2 suites raise tier-2 coverage to %d but they are table-driven placeholders, not authored tests. P1 criteria remain without any test and must be covered before their feature's phase exit.\n" % (len(lf) - len(lf_unc), len(lf), 100.0 * (len(lf) - len(lf_unc)) / len(lf), len(lf) - len(lf_unc) + lf_suite))
L.append("## 4. Suite-level coverage rule\n")
L.append("A criterion is *covered* for a Ready decision only when an individual test cites it. A criterion tied only by `TEST-ACC-*` is reported as *suite-covered* and may not be counted in the coverage headline. The suites are defined in `TEST_CATALOG.md#record-test-catalog-rev2` section 3.\n")
L.append("## 5. Per-feature acceptance-criteria counts\n")
L.append("Columns: AC = criteria whose id carries the feature number; AC tested = of those, directly cited by an individual test; FEAT tests = test rows citing the feature id itself (feature-level link).\n")
L.append("| Feature | Pri | Ph | AC | AC tested | FEAT tests | Flag |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
for f, p, ph, n, c, ft in feat_rows:
    flag = ""
    if n == 0 and p.startswith("P0"): flag = "BLOCKER: P0 without AC" + ("" if ft else " and without test")
    elif n == 0: flag = "no AC"
    elif c < n and p.startswith("P0"): flag = "P0 AC gap"
    L.append("| %s | %s | %s | %d | %d | %d | %s |" % (f, p, ph, n, c, ft, flag))
L.append("")
L.append("Features with at least one AC: %d of %d. Features with no AC: %d (P0: %d, of which %d have no feature-level test either).\n" % (len(feat_rows) - len(no_ac), len(feat_rows), len(no_ac), len(p0_no_ac), len(p0_noac_notest)))
L.append("### 5.1 Blockers to Ready: P0 features with no AC\n")
L.append("Rule (REVW-17): a P0 feature with no AC **and** no test cannot be Ready. A P0 feature with no AC but a feature-level test is marked *partial*; it still needs ACs before Ready.\n")
L.append("| Feature | Phase | State |\n|---|---|---|")
for f, p, ph, n, c, ft in p0_no_ac:
    L.append("| %s | %s | %s |" % (f, ph, "BLOCKER (no AC, no test)" if ft == 0 else "partial (feature-level test only)"))
L.append("\nNo long-form spec exists for DOM-02, 04, 12, 14, 17, 19, 20, 21, 23, 24 per the review; the requirements owners must write specs and ACs first for tenancy (DOM-02), AI gateway (DOM-17), moderation (DOM-19), admin (DOM-20) and platform plumbing (DOM-24). This is outside this pass.\n")
L.append("## 6. BR to AC to TEST\n")
L.append("| Status | Meaning | Count of %d BRs |\n|---|---|---|" % len(brs))
for k, m in (("A direct TEST", "an individual test cites the BR"), ("B via cited AC", "an AC citing the BR is directly tested"), ("C feature-level only", "only the BR's feature has tested ACs; link not explicit"), ("D none", "no path")):
    L.append("| %s | %s | %d |" % (k, m, st_cnt.get(k, 0)))
L.append("\nExplicit coverage (A+B) = %d of %d BRs (%.0f%%). Rules in C and D are tied only by the REV2 `TEST-BRU-*` block suites (suite-level).\n" % (st_cnt["A direct TEST"] + st_cnt["B via cited AC"], len(brs), 100.0 * (st_cnt["A direct TEST"] + st_cnt["B via cited AC"]) / len(brs)))
L.append("| BR | Scope | Features | Status | Direct tests | Via AC | Suite |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
for b, v in sorted(br_info.items()):
    L.append("| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |" % (b, brs[b]["scope"][:30], ", ".join(v["feats"][:4]) + ("..." if len(v["feats"]) > 4 else ""), v["status"], ", ".join(v["direct"][:4]), ", ".join(v["via_ac"][:3]), suite_of_br.get(b, "")))
L.append("")
L.append("## 7. Invariants and fitness functions\n")
L.append("INV-T uncited by any test row: %s. FF uncited: %s. (FF-11 and FF-12 gained tests TEST-API-016 and TEST-PRV-013 in REV2.)\n" % (", ".join(sorted(inv_unc)) or "none", ", ".join(ff_unc) or "none"))
L.append("## 8. Uncovered acceptance criteria (no individual test), by feature\n")
L.append("`S` = tied by a REV2 suite, `-` = no test of any tier (P1 features and AC-00).\n")
L.append("| Feature | Pri | Ph | Uncited ACs |\n|---|---|---|---|")
for f, lst in sorted(byf.items()):
    m = feats[f]
    L.append("| %s | %s | %s | %s |" % (f, m["pri"], m["phase"].strip(), ", ".join("%s%s" % (a, " S" if a in suite_of_ac else " -") for a in lst)))
for a in a00_unc:
    L.append("| AC-00 | - | - | %s - (register names %s) |" % (a, ", ".join(sorted(B.reverse_register.get(a, [])))))
L.append("")
L.append("Open items from this audit for the governance agent: WP-QA-TRACE must turn this script into a CI lint; the 26-versus-%d feature-without-AC discrepancy must be closed in TRC-01; the capability-map priority/phase columns must be regenerated from the roadmap (REVW-10) and this script re-run; AC-08-03-03 (lesson progress queued) is consistent with REVW-15, but depends on the outbox being activated (COND-OUTBOX) in Phase 2.\n" % len(no_ac))
L.append("## 9. Script source (reproducibility)\n")
for fn in ("trace_lib.py", "build_matrices.py", "generate_outputs.py"):
    L.append("### %s\n\n```python\n%s\n```\n" % (fn, pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name(fn).read_text(encoding="utf-8").rstrip()))
(ROOT / "28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/COVERAGE_MATRICES.md").write_text("\n".join(L), encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
print("suites", len(suite_rows), len(bru_rows), "ac unc", len(ac_unc), "suite-tied", len(suite_of_ac), "br", dict(st_cnt), "no_ac", len(no_ac), len(p0_no_ac), len(p0_noac_notest), "missing", missing)
````

## Feature Registry Metadata Crosswalk

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/FEATURE_REGISTRY_METADATA_CROSSWALK.md`._

Doc ID: TRC-03 · Status: Candidate preservation map; owner review required · Date: 2026-10-25

The protected root SSOT feature inventory and `docs/registries/FEATURE_REGISTRY.md` share 173 `F-*` identities, but the registry also carries source category, priority (`P`), phase, and dependency metadata. Those fields are not implementation status and do not automatically map to the target capability map's local priorities or Ph0–Ph4.

The [machine-readable crosswalk](#feature_registry_metadata_crosswalkcsv) has one row per `F-01…F-173`. It preserves the registry name/category/priority/phase/dependencies, the root SSOT feature name, and the candidate local FEAT IDs/match from [SSOT_FEATURE_CROSSWALK](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART4.md#canonical-ssot-feature-crosswalk-draft). This is a documentation preservation aid; product owners must decide whether source metadata remains useful and how it relates to target sequencing. OPEN-41 remains open for identity and metadata disposition.

Source `FEATURE_REGISTRY.md` states implementation status belongs to the dated feature-status report, not the canonical registry. Do not treat source priority or phase as current implementation readiness, and do not infer a migration or retirement from a blank candidate mapping.
